willed in an enduring abode supports satisfaction as fulfilled desire."},{"label":"medium","ref":"27:36","note":"Contrasts human gifts with what God has given, clarifying the source and rank of benefaction."},{"label":"strong","ref":"28:24","note":"An articulated need before the Lord directly supplies the request side of f04."},{"label":"strong","ref":"28:77","note":"What God has given is directed toward the later abode, closely joining gift and futurity."},{"label":"medium","ref":"32:17","note":"A concealed delight reserved as recompense extends the future satisfaction horizon."},{"label":"medium","ref":"33:35","note":"Forgiveness and immense reward for a formed response provide a future outcome parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"34:39","note":"What is given out is met by divine replacement, directly developing f03's circulation of giving."},{"label":"medium","ref":"35:30","note":"Full recompense and increase from bounty reinforce an abundant, delayed completion."},{"label":"medium","ref":"39:10","note":"Unmeasured recompense for patience supports the promise's expansive future horizon."},{"label":"medium","ref":"39:34","note":"Having what is desired with the Lord directly supports satisfaction as fulfilled desire."},{"label":"medium","ref":"42:22","note":"What is desired with the Lord extends the fulfilled-outcome reading in an explicit future setting."},{"label":"strong","ref":"43:32","note":"The Lord's distribution of livelihood and mercy strongly clarifies the giver's active provision."},{"label":"medium","ref":"47:17","note":"Guidance is accompanied by a bestowed capacity for reverence, extending giving beyond material form."},{"label":"medium","ref":"47:28","note":"Boundary: preferring what angers God and disliking approval negates the f02 route."},{"label":"medium","ref":"48:29","note":"A promise of forgiveness and immense reward supports the forward assurance after faithful action."},{"label":"medium","ref":"53:39","note":"Personal striving establishes a non-passive boundary for receiving the promised outcome."},{"label":"medium","ref":"53:41","note":"Full requital of striving supplies a delayed-completion parallel."},{"label":"medium","ref":"57:11","note":"Giving is met by multiplication and a noble reward, developing the reciprocal service reading."},{"label":"medium","ref":"57:21","note":"Forgiveness and immense bounty in a race toward the Lord support the future abundance reading."},{"label":"strong","ref":"57:28","note":"A double share of mercy and granted light closely parallel abundant divine provision."},{"label":"medium","ref":"59:8","note":"Seeking both bounty and approval directly holds f01 and f02 together."},{"label":"medium","ref":"62:4","note":"Divine bounty given by will supports the agency of the giver."},{"label":"strong","ref":"65:2","note":"A way out and provision beyond expectation strongly develop dependable lordly care."},{"label":"medium","ref":"65:3","note":"Provision from an unanticipated direction reinforces the non-limited character of giving."},{"label":"weak","ref":"71:12","note":"Material increase is a narrower provision parallel than the focus's satisfaction horizon."},{"label":"strong","ref":"76:22","note":"A declared recompense and appreciated striving closely parallel fulfilled satisfaction after giving."},{"label":"medium","ref":"87:17","note":"The later life is explicitly better and more lasting, reinforcing 93:4's temporal frame."},{"label":"medium","ref":"92:17","note":"Future rescue for the most reverent completes the contrast around the selfless giver."},{"label":"strong","ref":"92:18","note":"Purifying expenditure is the immediate missing premise for 92:19-21's satisfaction sequence."},{"label":"medium","ref":"94:5","note":"Ease accompanying hardship supplies a near-surah pattern of delayed relief."},{"label":"weak","ref":"94:6","note":"The repeated ease formula confirms 94:5 but adds little independently."}],"channel_subchannels_anchored_here":[{"parent_channel":"Temporal Passage and Emergence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Things occupy phases, move through ordered succession, and become manifest after absence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The day-night sequence extends into reversal, ontological emergence, fresh life, and sudden incident.","key":"D","name":"Freshness and Early Life","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A life or phase remains close to its beginning and carries the freshness of recent arrival.","active_motifs":"youth and recentness `ح د ث:B002/m01`; newly delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; fresh beginning or early youth `ر ب ب:B009/m02`; first phase `ء و ل:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"Recent birth, youth, and firstness converge on a scene whose defining state is proximity to origin. Freshness is therefore temporal as well as bodily.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Care, Need, and Provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.","parent_surprising_reach":"The surface sequence of rescue and command expands into a full economy of guardianship, sufficiency, fulfilled requests, and anti-neglect.","key":"A","name":"The Orphan Received into Care","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A child lacking a protector is found, sheltered, and raised under continuing care.","active_motifs":"orphaned child `ي ت م:B001/m01`; finding the vulnerable person `و ج د:B001/m01`; sheltering another `ء و ي:B001/m02`; gradual nurture `ر ب ب:B002/m02`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`","synthesis":"Orphanhood names the broken protective relation, finding brings the child into view, sheltering restores place and belonging, and gradual nurture turns a moment of rescue into sustained care.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:6","93:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Care, Need, and Provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.","parent_surprising_reach":"The surface sequence of rescue and command expands into a full economy of guardianship, sufficiency, fulfilled requests, and anti-neglect.","key":"B","name":"Poverty Changed into Sufficiency","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A person in need receives enough resources and capacity to leave dependence behind.","active_motifs":"poverty and need `ع ي ل:B001/m01`; poor nourishment `ع ي ل:B004/m01`; wealth or independence `غ ن ي:B001/m01`; sufficiency and useful adequacy `غ ن ي:B002/m01`; material capacity `و ج د:B003/m01`; good condition `ن ع م:B001/m01`; state or circumstance `ء و ل:B007/m01`; need `ر ب ب:B016/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`, `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`","synthesis":"Poverty and inadequate nourishment define the initial circumstance. Wealth, capacity, and practical sufficiency are distinct outcomes: possession increases, need falls, and the person becomes able to stand without dependence.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4","93:6","93:8"]},{"parent_channel":"Care, Need, and Provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.","parent_surprising_reach":"The surface sequence of rescue and command expands into a full economy of guardianship, sufficiency, fulfilled requests, and anti-neglect.","key":"C","name":"Request, Grant, and Gentle Reception","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A requester names a need, seeks a gift, receives a grant, and is not driven away by harsh speech.","active_motifs":"asking and seeking `س ء ل:B001/m01`; desired object `س ء ل:B002/m01`; fulfillment of a request `س ء ل:B003/m01`; giving `ع ط و:B002/m01`; solicitation for a gift `ع ط و:B005/m01`; generous gift `خ ي ر:B005/m01`; affectionate present `ه د ي:B004/m01`; harsh rebuke `ن ه ر:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ`, `تَنْهَرْ`","synthesis":"The requested object gives asking a goal, solicitation initiates transfer, giving and the affectionate present answer it, and fulfillment completes it. Harsh rebuke is the rejected counter-action that would break this exchange at its opening.","ayah_refs":["93:4","93:5","93:7","93:10"]},{"parent_channel":"Care, Need, and Provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.","parent_surprising_reach":"The surface sequence of rescue and command expands into a full economy of guardianship, sufficiency, fulfilled requests, and anti-neglect.","key":"D","name":"Stewardship against Coercion and Neglect","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A responsible holder governs and repairs what is under care instead of abandoning, ignoring, or overpowering it.","active_motifs":"lordship and responsible possession `ر ب ب:B001/m01`; governance and reform `ء و ل:B004/m01`; abandonment `و د ع:B001/m01`; neglect or omission `ي ت م:B003/m01`; refusal to touch or attend `س ج و:B004/m01`; coercive domination `ق ه ر:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`","synthesis":"Lordship and governance establish responsibility for another's condition. Abandonment, non-attendance, and neglect withdraw that responsibility, while coercion converts it into harmful power. Repair is the positive operation that opposes both failures.","ayah_refs":["93:2","93:3","93:4","93:5","93:11","93:6","93:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Household, Estate, and Custody","parent_semantic_invariant":"People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.","parent_surprising_reach":"Care expands from the orphan into family organization, foster relations, marriage, estate labor, and deposit custody.","key":"A","name":"Household, Dependents, and Service","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A household head gathers family and followers, maintains dependents, and assigns service for their needs.","active_motifs":"household and retinue `ء و ل:B003/m01`; dependents `ع ي ل:B002/m01`; service and handing what is needed `ع ط و:B003/m01`; labor placed at another's disposal `و د ع:B009/m01`; gathered multitude `ر ب ب:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`","synthesis":"The household supplies the social container, dependents supply those maintained within it, and service supplies the repeated transfer of labor and necessities. The multitude motif scales the same relation from one family to a gathered group.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4","93:8"]},{"parent_channel":"Household, Estate, and Custody","parent_semantic_invariant":"People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.","parent_surprising_reach":"Care expands from the orphan into family organization, foster relations, marriage, estate labor, and deposit custody.","key":"B","name":"Foster Parent and Ward","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A young dependent from an earlier household is received by a foster parent or nurse and raised as a ward.","active_motifs":"stepchild or ward `ر ب ب:B005/m01`; foster parent, nurse, or caregiver `ر ب ب:B005/m02`; orphaned child `ي ت م:B001/m01`; youthful recent age `ح د ث:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"Youth and orphanhood describe the ward's vulnerability, while the paired ward-caregiver senses supply a replacement relation of nurture. The scene centers on social incorporation rather than biological descent.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:6","93:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Household, Estate, and Custody","parent_semantic_invariant":"People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.","parent_surprising_reach":"Care expands from the orphan into family organization, foster relations, marriage, estate labor, and deposit custody.","key":"D","name":"Estate Repair and Directed Labor","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A holder directly manages an estate, repairs its condition, and deploys labor to keep it productive.","active_motifs":"direct management of an estate `س ج و:B005/m01`; estate ordering and repair `ء و ل:B004/m02`; repair, completion, and oversight `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; labor made available for a task `و د ع:B009/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`","synthesis":"Direct management identifies the setting, ordering supplies administrative control, repair restores the estate's condition, and deployed labor carries the work into practice.","ayah_refs":["93:2","93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Household, Estate, and Custody","parent_semantic_invariant":"People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.","parent_surprising_reach":"Care expands from the orphan into family organization, foster relations, marriage, estate labor, and deposit custody.","key":"E","name":"Deposit and Safekeeping","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Property is handed over, held under another's care, maintained, and returned at a later time.","active_motifs":"entrusted deposit `و د ع:B005/m01`; custodial oversight and repair `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; ordered estate keeping `ء و ل:B004/m02`; deferred transfer `ء خ ر:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ`, `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`","synthesis":"Deposit creates the custodial relation, oversight preserves the entrusted object, estate ordering keeps it within an accountable arrangement, and deferment explains why possession and final transfer occur at different times.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:4","93:5","93:11"]},{"parent_channel":"Covenant, Assent, and Power","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social relations are stabilized through pact, consent, protection, and surety, or ruptured through coercion and hostile dominance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Satisfaction unfolds into bilateral accord, legal guarantee, protected status, truce, and contested victory.","key":"A","name":"Pact, Truce, and Protected Entrant","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Parties suspend conflict through a pact and extend protected standing to a person entering their domain.","active_motifs":"covenant or pact `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; truce and mutual cessation `و د ع:B004/m01`; protected entrant or asylum seeker `ه د ي:B007/m01`; captive under another's control `ه د ي:B007/m02`","ayah_anchors":"93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`","synthesis":"The pact binds the parties, truce names the cessation it produces, and protected status applies that bond to a vulnerable entrant. The captive sense marks the neighboring condition in which protection and control must be distinguished.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Covenant, Assent, and Power","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social relations are stabilized through pact, consent, protection, and surety, or ruptured through coercion and hostile dominance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Satisfaction unfolds into bilateral accord, legal guarantee, protected status, truce, and contested victory.","key":"B","name":"Acceptance and Mutual Pleasing","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Aversion is removed, acceptance is established, and two parties make their approval reciprocal.","active_motifs":"acceptance opposed to displeasure `ر ض و:B001/m01`; abundant or sought satisfaction `ر ض و:B002/m01`; mutual accord `ر ض و:B003/m01`; active pleasing of another `ر ض و:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`","synthesis":"Acceptance supplies an inward state, sought satisfaction makes it a goal, pleasing makes one party act toward the other, and mutual accord closes the relation bilaterally.","ayah_refs":["93:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Covenant, Assent, and Power","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social relations are stabilized through pact, consent, protection, and surety, or ruptured through coercion and hostile dominance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Satisfaction unfolds into bilateral accord, legal guarantee, protected status, truce, and contested victory.","key":"C","name":"Obedience, Attachment, and Surety","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A trusted subordinate is attached to a principal through willing obedience, affection, or guarantee.","active_motifs":"obedient trusted person `ر ض و:B006/m01`; loving attached person `ر ض و:B006/m02`; guarantor or surety `ر ض و:B006/m03`; lord or principal `ر ب ب:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`","synthesis":"The principal supplies the relation's authoritative pole. Obedience realizes it behaviorally, attachment realizes it affectively, and surety realizes it as an obligation undertaken for another.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11"]},{"parent_channel":"Covenant, Assent, and Power","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social relations are stabilized through pact, consent, protection, and surety, or ruptured through coercion and hostile dominance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Satisfaction unfolds into bilateral accord, legal guarantee, protected status, truce, and contested victory.","key":"D","name":"Coercion, Contest, and Cessation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Opponents drive, seize, or overpower one another until conflict is restrained by cessation.","active_motifs":"coercive domination `ق ه ر:B001/m01`; victory in contest `ع ط و:B007/m01`; overcoming a rival `ر ض و:B005/m01`; expulsion or driving out `ق ل ي:B002/m01`; stealthy snatching `ن ه ر:B006/m01`; armistice `و د ع:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `قَلَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`, `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`","synthesis":"Driving and snatching initiate dispossession, contest turns it into reciprocal struggle, and coercion fixes a victor over a subdued party. Armistice is the social mechanism that interrupts this chain without requiring either side's annihilation.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:9","93:10"]},{"parent_channel":"Voice, Dialogue, and Public Memory","parent_semantic_invariant":"Speech moves between participants, discloses what matters, and can persist as interpretation, reputation, song, or remembered account.","parent_surprising_reach":"The command to recount expands into dialogue, public reputation, learned interpretation, and performed verse.","key":"A","name":"Question, Answer, and Mutual Consultation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Participants ask one another, answer affirmatively, exchange news, and reach an agreed response.","active_motifs":"reciprocal questioning `س ء ل:B004/m01`; affirmative answer `ن ع م:B004/m01`; renewed speech or news `ح د ث:B003/m01`; mutual accord `ر ض و:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`, `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"Reciprocal questioning opens the exchange, renewed speech supplies its content, affirmation answers it, and mutual accord gives the conversation a shared outcome.","ayah_refs":["93:5","93:10","93:11"]},{"parent_channel":"Voice, Dialogue, and Public Memory","parent_semantic_invariant":"Speech moves between participants, discloses what matters, and can persist as interpretation, reputation, song, or remembered account.","parent_surprising_reach":"The command to recount expands into dialogue, public reputation, learned interpretation, and performed verse.","key":"B","name":"Telling the Benefaction","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A received good is made visible by being recounted as news.","active_motifs":"narrated speech `ح د ث:B003/m01`; manifestation and showing `ح د ث:B006/m01`; bestowed benefaction `ن ع م:B001/m02`; generous gift `خ ي ر:B005/m01`; lordly giver `ر ب ب:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`, `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"Benefaction supplies the content, the giver supplies its relational source, narration turns it into speech, and manifestation lets the spoken good become publicly perceptible.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Voice, Dialogue, and Public Memory","parent_semantic_invariant":"Speech moves between participants, discloses what matters, and can persist as interpretation, reputation, song, or remembered account.","parent_surprising_reach":"The command to recount expands into dialogue, public reputation, learned interpretation, and performed verse.","key":"C","name":"Conduct Becoming Public Memory","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A person's course of action is repeatedly discussed until it becomes a remembered story or reputation.","active_motifs":"becoming an anecdote or public account `ح د ث:B004/m01`; course and manner `ه د ي:B002/m01`; lapse or omission `ي ت م:B003/m01`; gathered public `ر ب ب:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"Conduct supplies the subject of talk, a lapse supplies a memorable deviation, the gathered public repeats it, and repeated speech converts the person or event into an enduring anecdote.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:6","93:9","93:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Voice, Dialogue, and Public Memory","parent_semantic_invariant":"Speech moves between participants, discloses what matters, and can persist as interpretation, reputation, song, or remembered account.","parent_surprising_reach":"The command to recount expands into dialogue, public reputation, learned interpretation, and performed verse.","key":"D","name":"Interpretation as Return to Meaning","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Speech is carried back to its intended sense under learned direction.","active_motifs":"interpretation by return to meaning `ء و ل:B002/m02`; speech or report `ح د ث:B003/m01`; directed course of discourse `ه د ي:B002/m01`; learned teacher or sage `ر ب ب:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`","synthesis":"A report presents language that can travel away from its intended point. Interpretation returns it to that point, while the learned guide supplies the disciplined course by which the return is made.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:4","93:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Residence, Ease, and Composure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A participant settles into a fitting place or bodily state and gains stability, comfort, and quiet bearing.","parent_surprising_reach":"Shelter becomes both a physical abode and a manner of settled softness, flexibility, and dignity.","key":"A","name":"Shelter and Agreeable Abode","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A person joins a shelter, remains there, and finds the place sufficient and agreeable for habitation.","active_motifs":"joining a refuge `ء و ي:B001/m01`; lasting residence `ر ب ب:B007/m01`; inhabited abode `غ ن ي:B004/m01`; agreeable place and pleasant stay `ن ع م:B011/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:8 `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`","synthesis":"Refuge supplies entry, residence supplies duration, the abode supplies location, and agreeableness turns mere occupancy into a fitting home.","ayah_refs":["93:3","93:5","93:11","93:6","93:8"]},{"parent_channel":"Residence, Ease, and Composure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A participant settles into a fitting place or bodily state and gains stability, comfort, and quiet bearing.","parent_surprising_reach":"Shelter becomes both a physical abode and a manner of settled softness, flexibility, and dignity.","key":"B","name":"Rest, Softness, and Quiet Bearing","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Effort subsides, the body or material yields without resistance, and the participant holds a calm, dignified posture.","active_motifs":"repose and ease `و د ع:B002/m01`; softness and refined comfort `ن ع م:B002/m01`; calm bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; eyelid stillness `س ج و:B001/m04`; flexibility and yielding `ع ط و:B006/m01`","ayah_anchors":"93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`","synthesis":"Repose removes exertion, softness and flexibility remove resistance, eyelid stillness gives the calm a bodily sign, and composed bearing makes comfort socially visible as dignity.","ayah_refs":["93:2","93:3","93:5","93:7","93:11"]},{"parent_channel":"Weather, Water, and Navigation","parent_semantic_invariant":"Air, cloud, light, and flowing water form an environment through which land, vegetation, and travelers are sustained or directed.","parent_surprising_reach":"Night stillness and daylight open into cloud layers, south wind, river cutting, persistent greenery, calm sea, and maritime command.","key":"A","name":"Layered Cloud, Soft Wind, and Green Persistence","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Clouds gather and linger, a soft moist wind moves beneath them, and vegetation remains green.","active_motifs":"layered raincloud `ر ب ب:B008/m01`; ifade, anlamın merkezini hoşnutsuzluk ve öfkenin karşıtı olan hoşnutlukta kurar; bir şeyi veya kişiyi kabul etme ile kendisinden hoşnut olunan tarafı da bu merkeze bağlar. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
